# SoduWork client directory and file guide

**English** · [简体中文](DIRECTORY-GUIDE.zh-CN.md)

## 1. Architecture and reading order

The client uses Electron and native HTML/CSS/JavaScript. React/Vite administration lives in `hub-frontend/`; Hub and Runner source lives in `hub-backend/`.

Read `package.json`, `main.js`, `preload.js`, `desk.html`, `desk-boot.js`, then `components/workspace/` and `components/panels/`. HTML script ordering defines dependencies.

```text
main.js → main/ native services and execution runtimes
   ↕ restricted IPC via preload.js and window.soduWorkAPI
Workspace: desk.html → desk-boot.js → components/ + core/ + theme/
Pet overlay: index.html → app/app.js → components/pet/
api.js → Hub HTTP / WebSocket
panels-chat → conversationSession → IPC → agent-runtime → Pi / Runner
```

`main/conversation/` owns runtime-neutral conversation services and contracts; `main/agent-runtime/` handles adapters and process hosting; `main/pi-session/` implements session lifecycle and the historical session bridge. Its name alone does not imply that all callers use Pi.

## 2. New-chat home map

| Area | Main files |
| --- | --- |
| Heading and subtitle | `components/panels/compose-surface.js` |
| New-chat orchestration | `components/panels/panels-compose.js` |
| Shared input and toolbar | `components/panels/work-composer.js` and `.css` |
| Navigation shell | `components/workspace/workspace-shell.js` |
| Projects | `components/workspace/workspace-sidebar-projects.js` |
| Recent chats | `components/workspace/workspace-chat-list.js` and `workspace-chat-history.js` |
| Navigation/layout | `workspace-navigation.css`, `workspace-shell.css`, `workspace-page-layout.css` under `components/workspace/` |
| Sign-in | `components/client-auth/` |
| Theme | `theme/variables.css`, `theme/workspace-experience.css` |

## 3. Naming and file types

- `.js` may execute in the renderer or main process; check its loading entry.
- `.cjs` denotes CommonJS; `.mjs` denotes ES modules.
- `.css` and `.html` provide styles and markup.
- `ipc`, `store`, `contract` and `worker` identify interfaces, persistence, contracts and background execution helpers. Check the caller to distinguish worker threads from child processes.
- Tests use `.test.js` or `.test.cjs`; fixtures contain test input. `vendor/` contains separately licensed libraries.

## 5. Startup, checks and generated resources

From the repository root run `sh start.sh --desktop`. For independent client development, keep Hub running, then run `npm ci` and `npm start` inside `client/`. Node.js 22.19+ is required. Use `npm test` for client unit/contract checks; inspect fixture requirements before running `e2e/` scripts.

`package.json` defines Electron packaging and includes product source while excluding tests. `scripts/` prepares Node, Python, Pi, shell runtime and Runner; `build/` implements installer behavior. `runner/` contains generated executables; their source is in the backend. `.runtime/`, `dist/` and `node_modules/` may appear after preparation and are not maintained source. User profiles, credentials and databases belong outside this source tree.

The root launcher uses a managed application copy under its private data directory. Editing this checkout does not modify the already running copy. Restart with the same source launcher to prepare the current source; do not edit that managed copy directly.

## 6. Implementation boundaries confirmed in source

| Modules | Content-based boundary |
| --- | --- |
| Main, preload and Hub API | `main.js` wires native services; preload exposes `window.soduWorkAPI`; the renderer Hub facade is `window.SoduWorkAPI`. The different capitalization matters. |
| Local user and account authentication | `core/user.js` stores a local device ID/display name. `main/device-auth.js` owns Hub-scoped login identity, sessions and OS-encrypted credentials. |
| NativeChat and controllers | `panels-chat.js` owns conversation state; extracted controllers cooperate through state accessors rather than maintaining duplicate sessions. |
| Legacy details | `panels-detail.js` is compatibility routing. Requirement facts/delivery live in the requirement drawer; conversations remain in NativeChat. |
| Cloud view | `cloud-conversation-view.js` passively projects Hub tasks. Reopening it does not initialize a local model. Handoff/start services handle actual transitions. |
| Local and managed models | The local model store encrypts device-owned catalogs; session configuration prepares model settings and temporary tickets. Renderers do not own long-lived device secrets. |
| Automation | The worker hands off immutable triggers and receipts; ordinary chat execution owns the work. It is not a separate chat engine. |
| Hosted SDK | The SDK sends requests from controlled hosts/authorized iframes. Host bridges enforce policy without exposing arbitrary filesystem paths or Node access. |
| Outputs versus delivery | Workspace changes are evidence. Delivery selection, fixed versions, packaging and authorized actions are separate responsibilities. |
| Runner directory | Contains generated-output documentation; executor source and frozen-build inputs live in the backend. |
| PNG versus SVG | The current workspace shell constructs SVG rail paths. Neighboring PNG files alone do not prove current usage. Skin images are selected dynamically by state/locale. |
| Tests, e2e and fixtures | Scripts use different layers, including real Electron, disposable services and page fixtures. Reading them does not establish execution or success. |

## 7. Method and index conventions

- Every file has a content record; tests, licenses, examples, assets and third-party libraries remain distinct from business modules.
- Responsibilities use reviewed implementation summaries or explicit source comments. Evidence identifies real definitions, IPC, fields, tests, selectors or graphic structure.
- Imports and reverse references include resolved local `require`, `import from` and HTML resource declarations only. Dynamic injection, path templates and IPC events are not a complete dependency graph.
- Literal test-title counts are static observations, not executed test totals; generated or dynamic titles may be absent. Rows show representative samples.
- Images and minified third-party libraries are described using asset content, provenance, public interfaces and references; third-party algorithms were not audited line by line.
- These two generated guides and generated `node_modules/`, `.runtime/` and `dist/` directories are excluded. Runtime code, accounts and user data were not changed.
